@pytest.mark.parametrize("tags, system, frame, suffix", SYSTEMS)
def test_every_other_audit_cell_is_the_fixed_zone_jobs(tmp_path, tags, system, frame, suffix):
    """Strip the two county cells from an Auto row and it is the fixed-zone
    job's row of the point's zone, cell for cell - except the Warnings cell
    where the fixed job warned OUTSIDE_ZONE_EXTENT, which Auto never does."""
    result, archive = auto_job(tmp_path, tags, system, frame)
    audit = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(members_of(archive)[f"pts_{suffix}_full.csv"])))
    at = audit[0].index("County")
    stripped = [row[:at] + row[at + 2 :] for row in audit]
    for index, code in enumerate(expected_zones(tags, system)):
        zone = zone_by_code(code)
        fixed = run(dataclasses.replace(result.settings, target_zone=zone, auto_frame=None))
        fixed_rows = exports.audit_rows(fixed)
        assert stripped[0] == fixed_rows[0]
        warnings = fixed_rows[0].index("Warnings")
        fixed_row = list(fixed_rows[index + 1])
        if fixed_row[warnings] == "outside-zone-extent":
            fixed_row[warnings] = ""
        assert stripped[index + 1] == fixed_row


def test_a_fixed_zone_audit_has_no_county_columns(reviewers_result):
    fixed = run(dataclasses.replace(reviewers_result.settings, target_zone=MI_SOUTH, auto_frame=None))
    assert "County" not in exports.audit_rows(fixed)[0]
    assert "County FIPS" not in exports.audit_rows(fixed)[0]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. The record.
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _block(record: str, first: str, stop: str) -> str:
    start = record.index(first)
    return record[start : record.index(stop, start)]


TO_BLOCK_83 = """\
TO: Auto zone - each point in the zone of its county (SPCS 83)
  Reference frame           NAD83(2011)
  Zones used                3 of the 3 SPCS 83 zones Auto chooses among
    Michigan North, zone 2111 (MI-N): 1 point
      in pts_AUTO-SPCS83_MI-N.csv
    Michigan Central, zone 2112 (MI-C): 2 points
      in pts_AUTO-SPCS83_MI-C.csv
    Michigan South, zone 2113 (MI-S): 2 points
      in pts_AUTO-SPCS83_MI-S.csv

Zone rule          Each point is placed in the zone of the Michigan county its
                   NAD83(2011) position lies in. The counties of each zone are
                   those named by MCL 54.231(3)-(5), Act 9 of 1964 as amended
                   by 1988 PA 154 (Imd. Eff. June 14, 1988), captured from
                   legislature.mi.gov 2026-10-22: subsection (3) the north
                   zone, (4) the central zone, (5) the south zone.
County data        US Census TIGER/Line 2025 counties, all 83 of Michigan's,
                   whose lines extend into the Great Lakes, so a position on
                   the water is in a county as a position on land is. Bundled
                   as mi_counties_tiger2025.json, checked against SHA-256
                   53664dd816375b407c5269cabde565fc89d76e0a3f3ed320fb264240b49f33c3
                   when read.
On a line          A position exactly on a county line is placed in the county
                   east of it, or, where the line runs exactly east-west, in
                   the county north of it.
Near a line        A point near a zone line is placed by its county like every
                   other point, with no warning. MCL 54.234 provides that a
                   tract of land defined by a single description and extending
                   from one zone into another may have the positions of all
                   points on its boundaries referred to either zone, and that
                   the zone used shall be named in the description. Each
                   point's zone and county are in the _full.csv export.

"""

TO_BLOCK_2022 = """\
TO: Auto zone - each point in the zone of its county (SPCS2022)
  Reference frame           NATRF2022
  Zones used                7 of the 18 SPCS2022 zones Auto chooses among
    Michigan Thunder Bay, zone 261006 (MI_L35T): 1 point
      in pts_AUTO-SPCS2022_MI_L35T.csv
    Michigan Kalamazoo, zone 261007 (MI_L41Z): 1 point
      in pts_AUTO-SPCS2022_MI_L41Z.csv
    Michigan Grand Rapids, zone 261008 (MI_L45G): 1 point
      in pts_AUTO-SPCS2022_MI_L45G.csv
    Michigan Leelanau, zone 261011 (MI_L61L): 1 point
      in pts_AUTO-SPCS2022_MI_L61L.csv
    Michigan Escanaba, zone 261014 (MI_U21E): 1 point
      in pts_AUTO-SPCS2022_MI_U21E.csv
    Michigan Houghton, zone 261016 (MI_U41H): 1 point
      in pts_AUTO-SPCS2022_MI_U41H.csv
    Michigan Isle Royale, zone 261018 (MI_U61K): 1 point
      in pts_AUTO-SPCS2022_MI_U61K.csv

<ZONE RULE>
Isle Royale        A Keweenaw County position at or north of 47.65 degrees N
                   is placed in Michigan Isle Royale, zone 261018 (MI_U61K);
                   south of it, in Michigan Houghton, zone 261016 (MI_U41H).
                   This is this program's rule. NGS publishes no text stating
                   it; NGS's zone bounds file (zoneBounds.json) gives the two
                   zones a shared bound at 47.65 degrees N.
County data        US Census TIGER/Line 2025 counties, all 83 of Michigan's,
                   whose lines extend into the Great Lakes, so a position on
                   the water is in a county as a position on land is. Bundled
                   as mi_counties_tiger2025.json, checked against SHA-256
                   53664dd816375b407c5269cabde565fc89d76e0a3f3ed320fb264240b49f33c3
                   when read.
On a line          A position exactly on a county line is placed in the county
                   east of it, or, where the line runs exactly east-west, in
                   the county north of it.
Near a line        A point near a zone line is placed by its county like every
                   other point, with no warning. Each point's zone and county
                   are in the _full.csv export.

"""


def test_the_spcs83_to_block_reads_exactly(tmp_path):
    """Hand-written. The zones and counts are NCAT's for the five anchors:
    Alger -> 2111 (1); Alcona, Benzie -> 2112 (2); Calhoun, Allegan -> 2113 (2)."""
    _, archive = auto_job(tmp_path, TAGS_83, "SPCS 83", NAD83_2011)
    record = members_of(archive)["pts_AUTO-SPCS83_README.txt"]
    assert _block(record, "TO: Auto zone", "TO (Auto): ") == TO_BLOCK_83


def test_the_spcs2022_to_block_reads_exactly(tmp_path):
    """Hand-written. Seven anchors, seven zones (NCAT's), one point each."""
    _, archive = auto_job(tmp_path, TAGS_2022, "SPCS2022", NATRF2022)
    record = members_of(archive)["pts_AUTO-SPCS2022_README.txt"]
    block = _block(record, "TO: Auto zone", "TO (Auto): ")
    before, rule, after = TO_BLOCK_2022.partition("<ZONE RULE>\n")
    assert rule
    assert block.startswith(before) and block.endswith(after)
    # The Zone rule paragraph, read as words: hand-written up to the source,
    # which is the table module's own citation of the NGS capture it was
    # measured from (that module's provenance text is pinned with it, in
    # tests/test_autozone_tables.py), quoted whole.
    paragraph = block[len(before) : len(block) - len(after)]
    assert paragraph.startswith("Zone rule          Each point is placed")
    assert " ".join(paragraph.split()) == (
        "Zone rule Each point is placed in the low-distortion zone of the "
        "Michigan county its NATRF2022 position lies in; the statewide zone "
        "260001 is never chosen. NGS publishes no county list for its SPCS2022 "
        "zones. This program measured one from NGS's zone polygons: each zone "
        "is a union of whole Census TIGER counties, except Keweenaw County, "
        "whose Isle Royale is its own zone. Source: "
        + " ".join(NGS_POLYGONS_CITATION.split())
        + "."
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("tags, system, frame, suffix", SYSTEMS)
def test_each_used_zone_is_described_and_no_other(tmp_path, tags, system, frame, suffix):
    _, archive = auto_job(tmp_path, tags, system, frame)
    record = members_of(archive)[f"pts_{suffix}_README.txt"]
    described = [line for line in record.split("\n") if line.startswith("TO (Auto): ")]
    used = sorted(set(expected_zones(tags, system)), key=int)
    assert described == [
        f"TO (Auto): {zone_by_code(c).name}, zone {c} ({zone_by_code(c).system})" for c in used
    ]
    # The fixed-zone TO heading never appears.
    assert not [line for line in record.split("\n") if line.startswith("TO: Michigan")]


def test_the_spcs2022_record_is_in_the_spcs2022_era(tmp_path):
    _, archive = auto_job(tmp_path, TAGS_2022, "SPCS2022", NATRF2022)
    record = members_of(archive)["pts_AUTO-SPCS2022_README.txt"]
    assert "Sixty-three positions spanning all nineteen Michigan" in " ".join(record.split())
    # The projections the seven zones use: the L zones are one-parallel
    # Lambert, U41H, U21E and U61K transverse Mercator (NGS's own kinds).
    kinds = {zone_by_code(c).projection_kind for c in expected_zones(TAGS_2022, "SPCS2022")}
    for kind, section in ((k, report._MANUAL_SECTIONS[k]) for k in kinds):
        assert f"section {section}  " in record
    for spcs83_sentence in (
        "Twenty-seven",
        "Appendix C, the constants NGS computed",
        "MCL 54.231",
        "MCL 54.234",
        "GRS 80, the ellipsoid of NAD 83 (manual section 1.7).",
    ):
        assert spcs83_sentence not in record


def test_the_spcs83_record_is_in_the_spcs83_era(tmp_path):
    _, archive = auto_job(tmp_path, TAGS_83, "SPCS 83", NAD83_2011)
    record = members_of(archive)["pts_AUTO-SPCS83_README.txt"]
    assert "Twenty-seven" in record
    for spcs2022_sentence in ("Sixty-three", "NGS publishes no county list", "Isle Royale        "):
        assert spcs2022_sentence not in record


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
        4.2). The combos keep whatever answer they held, so toggling modes
        does not silently discard a chosen datum or a chosen To zone - but a
        job that does not consult a hidden control never reads it:
        ``settings`` states None for a horizontal job's datums, and a
        vertical-only job never reads the To zone or the output unit.
        """
        mode = self.vertical_mode()
        for widget in (
            self.vertical_source_label,
            self.vertical_source_combo,
            self.vertical_target_label,
            self.vertical_target_combo,
            # The input-side geoid row is vertical-mode furniture exactly
            # as the datum rows it filters by are; within the vertical
            # modes a side with no models is GRAYED by
            # _refresh_geoid_sides, never hidden (the owner's distinction,
            # 2026-08-09).
            self.input_geoid_label,
            self.input_geoid_combo,
        ):
            widget.setVisible(mode.converts_elevations)
        self._refresh_geoid_sides()
        # The elevation hint is HORIZONTAL-ONLY, and this line is why #51
        # cannot happen twice: in the two vertical modes the elevation is the
        # value being converted, so "optional" would be false, and a
        # placeholder is a more prominent place to be false than a tooltip
        # was.
        self._update_elevation_hint()
        vertical_only = mode is VerticalMode.VERTICAL
        for widget in (
            self.to_zone_label,
            self.to_zone,
            self.output_unit_label,
            self.output_unit,
        ):
            widget.setVisible(not vertical_only)

    def entering_dms(self) -> bool:
        """True when the two coordinate rows are showing their four-box page.

        Both conditions, not just the dropdown: a northing has no minutes, so
        the DMS page is never shown while the job starts from a zone however
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _set_clipboard(self, text: str) -> None:
        """The one route to the clipboard. Overridden in tests, which run
        headless and must not depend on a platform clipboard."""
        QGuiApplication.clipboard().setText(text)

    def copy_value(self, index: int) -> bool:
        """Put exactly one displayed value on the clipboard.

        The value string alone: no label, no unit, no trailing newline. It is
        pasted straight into a CAD prompt or a spreadsheet cell, and anything
        else in the buffer has to be deleted there.
        """
        if self.sections is None:
            # Nothing has been converted, so there is nothing to copy. Refused
            # rather than copying an empty string, which would silently destroy
            # whatever the user already had on the clipboard.
            return False
        self._set_clipboard(self.panel.values[index].text)
        return True

    def copy_all(self) -> bool:
        """Put the whole result on the clipboard, labels included.

        Serialises the *same* sections tuple the panel rendered, through
        ``results_model.single_point_clipboard_text``, so the screen and the
        clipboard cannot disagree about what the result contains.
        """
        if self.sections is None:
            return False
        self._set_clipboard(single_point_clipboard_text(self.sections))
        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Reporting
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def status_text(self, result: JobResult) -> str:
        """"Converted. 1 warning." — one point, so the count is of warnings.

        Every warning the warnings field shows, the job's own included
        (``results_model.warning_count``): a realization-period warning in the
        self._update_longitude_relevance()
        self._update_observation_date_relevance()
        self._update_convert_enabled()

    def _update_unit_offerings(self) -> None:
        """Offer each end only the units its own selection publishes.

        The one owner of these two combos' item lists, so nothing else rebuilds
        them (the #57 defect was two methods driving one property, and this is
        the same shape one property along). ``controls`` owns the rule -
        ``units_for_selection`` reads ``Zone.allowed_units``, the authoritative
        statement - and ``job._require_units_the_zones_publish`` enforces the
        identical tuple on the settings, so this narrowing is a convenience
        over the gate and never a second rule.

        Neither combo is ever disabled here; see ``refresh_unit_combo``.

        **A forced swap discards the table.** SPCS2022 publishes metres and
        international feet only, so a job left on US survey feet has its unit
        changed for it when a 2022 zone is chosen - by the program, not by the
        user - and the table above would then be showing an archive's numbers
        under a unit selector that no longer describes them. That is the
        amendment #26 / #43 stale-display class reaching this tab through a
        control nobody touched.
        """
        snapped = refresh_unit_combo(
            self.input_unit, units_for_selection(self.from_zone.currentData())
        )
        snapped |= refresh_unit_combo(
            self.output_unit, units_for_selection(self.to_zone.currentData())
        )
        if snapped and self._clear_table():
            self._set_status(UNITS_SNAPPED_STATUS)

    def _clear_table(self) -> bool:
        """Empty the results table. True if something was on screen.

        The mechanism only; the caller says WHY, because the two reasons this
        tab clears its table are different sentences - a unit the program
        swapped, and a selection the program cleared. One clearing, two
        messages, rather than two clearings that could drift apart.

        **Narrower than the Single point tab's ``_invalidate_result``, on
        purpose.** This tab's table describes an archive that was WRITTEN, not
        the current controls, so it does not clear when a control moves - the
        files on disk are unchanged and their own record states the units they
        were written in. It clears for the two cases where leaving it would
        show numbers the controls above them contradict: a unit the program
        itself swapped, and a selection the program itself cleared.

        "Open folder" is disarmed with the table because it points at the run
        the table described. Nothing on disk is touched - the archive is still
        there and still correct - and the unit message says that outright.

        Idempotent: costs nothing when there is no result on screen, which is
        what the return value is for.
        """
        if self.result is None and self.model.rowCount() == 0:
            return False
        self.result = None
        self.written_files = {}
        self.model.set_result(None)
        self.open_folder_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.status_label.setToolTip("")
        return True

### The finding that changed the plan, and the one still open

**Plan §2.5 is superseded (§2.5a).** It said `.trn` is biquadratic and `.err`
bilinear, and asked for a pin that fails if the two are unified. **That pin would
have enshrined a defect.** `ngs_grid.interpolate_biquadratic` anchors its 3×3
stencil at `int(row) - 1`, off-centre; both VERTCON grids want it centred on the
nearest node. Then both are biquadratic and both land at **0.47 mm** against NCAT,
20/20 exact, where the old scheme reaches 8.46 mm. **Verified bit-identical to
NOAA's own published algorithm** — `Vertcon.java`'s `getGridRow` transcribed
literally, max difference exactly 0.0 over 18,000 positions.

**DESIGN.md #8 is corrected.** It said NGS does not document INTG's scheme. NGS
does: NOAA TM NOS NGS-84, and `intg.f` anchors with `nint()` — nearest node. So
**GEOID18's anchoring is not INTG's**, a claim three docstrings and the 0.1.0
release notes carried.

**OWNER'S INSTRUCTION, 2026-08-07: replicate NOAA as closely as possible, they
are the authority.** That decides the open question — **GEOID18 is to be
re-anchored to nearest-node**, as its own work package, because it touches
released code and needs its own discriminating anchors (the existing 20 cannot
tell the schemes apart; 120 points sampled where they diverge give floor rms
0.715 mm against nearest-node 0.454). Cost of the current anchoring is ~4 mm in a
*reported geoid separation* and ~6e-10 in an elevation factor — **no coordinate
moves** — which is why it was not folded into a vertical-datum build.

**STILL OPEN, and it is a disclosure decision for WP-V7:** the `.err` grid
interpolates **negative** at ~0.43% of Michigan positions (956 of 223,850 sampled,
worst −0.027 m). A negative one-sigma is not a quantity. **NOAA's own published
code produces the same negatives; NOAA's live NCAT service returns a positive
value there** (+0.011 m where we compute −0.00965 m), and no rule maps one to the
other — not `abs()`, not clamping. So `sigma_m` **refuses**, `modeled_error_raw_m`
keeps the raw value readable under a name that cannot be mistaken for an
uncertainty, and **the shift is unaffected and still reported**. All three
paper-overs are pinned as failures.

from −0.1466 and 249% at the WP-V4 gate; that point is an exact grid node, so the

### Round four (DESIGN.md #30) — warnings move out; display punctuation

1. **Warnings are a full-width field beneath the results panel**, with no copy
   button and out of Copy all. `single_point_sections` no longer builds the
   row, so the clipboard drops it without a special case; the text comes from
   the new `single_point_warnings` over the same `_warnings_text`.
2. **A defect found by looking, not by a test:** a wrapping `QLabel` does not
   propagate height-for-width out of a `QGroupBox`, so the field showed ONE
   line of a three-warning run and clipped the rest. Fixed with a bounded
   scroll area; pinned by measuring the label against its own content.
3. **Decimal lat/long carry `°`; convergence reads `-16°49'17.78"`.** These are
   **display-only** formatters built on top of the file ones. The symbol cannot
   go in `formatting.latitude`/`longitude`: they write the clean PNEZD export,
   which is read back before the archive is committed, so a symbol there would
  interim gate returned seven LOW findings; the second two HIGH, one MEDIUM
  and five LOW; the closing gate one MEDIUM and seven LOW. None was a wrong
  number. All are fixed, each pinned at the reviewer's own input, and each
  gate's fixes passed a narrowing re-confirmation. The closing gate's
  reviewer drove the real interface on both tabs, 2,050 Single point
  configurations and 540 Multi point jobs, against its own HTDP build with
  no mismatch.
- **The Options and Auto zone part's interim gate ran under Codex** over the
  county data, the zone lookup and Auto in the conversion
  (`review/gate-autozone-1/`). It found no wrong zone and returned one HIGH
  and one MEDIUM. The HIGH: two public routines that turn a result into file
  text could still produce an Auto job's CAD file, with points from
  different zones in one unlabelled file, getting around the guard that held
  Auto jobs back until their job record existed. The guard was replaced by
  the real Auto output, and the CAD file routine now requires each point's
  zone on an Auto job and refuses a zone the job did not use, so points from
  two zones cannot reach one file. The MEDIUM: the SPCS2022 table's test read a
  copy of NGS's polygons that NGS's server had shifted about 0.9 m, while the
  agreement it cited was measured on the unshifted capture; the test now
  reads the unshifted capture. Both are fixed and pinned at the reviewer's

    settings: JobSettings, frame_path: FrameTransformation | None
) -> tuple[htdp.ObservationEpoch | None, tuple[ConversionWarning, ...]]:
    """The job's observation epoch and its job-level warnings, or a refusal.

    Plan decisions D9 and D10, all before the file is read:

    * the type: a ``datetime.date`` and nothing else. A ``datetime.datetime``
      IS a ``datetime.date`` to Python and is refused by name, because HTDP's
      calendar entry has no time of day and the time would be dropped without
      a word;
    * required when the path reads a time-dependent frame, refused by name
      when it does not (the stray-field class);
    * not after the day the job runs (``today``): an observation cannot be in
      the future, and HTDP would compute one without complaint;
    * HTDP's own year rule (``htdp.ObservationEpoch``: after 1906, within its
      minute clock), refused in HTDP's words;
    * a WARNING, not a refusal, when the date falls OUTSIDE the stated WGS 84
      realization's period of use (``frames.realization_period_of_use``,
      derived from the one table of start dates: each period ends the day
      before the next realization began). Reprocessed data can legitimately
      carry a later realization than the one broadcast that day, and software
      can keep labelling with an older one. No warning for
      ITRF, which has no NGA period of use. Raised once for the job
      (``JobResult.job_warnings``), because one date covers every point.
      Worded per direction: on a path INTO NAD83(2011) the realization is the
      input's label, on a path OUT of it the output the job was asked for
      (interim gate 2, finding L2).
    """
    date = settings.observation_date
    if date is not None and (
        isinstance(date, datetime.datetime) or not isinstance(date, datetime.date)
    ):
        raise TypeError(
            f"JobSettings.observation_date must be a datetime.date - the "
            f"calendar date the positions were observed - and nothing else; "
            f"got {type(date).__name__} ({date!r}). "
            + (
                "A datetime carries a time of day, and HTDP's calendar entry "
                "means 00:00 UTC on the date, so the time would be dropped "
                "without a word. Pass its .date()."
                if isinstance(date, datetime.datetime)
                else "A decimal year or a string is not accepted: the date is "
                "converted the way HTDP converts a calendar date "
                "(docs/PLAN-wgs84.md section 1, the owner's decision 3)."
            )
        )

    source = settings.observation_date_source
    if source is not None and not isinstance(source, ObservationDateSource):
        raise TypeError(
            f"JobSettings.observation_date_source must be an "
            f"ObservationDateSource - TODAY or ENTERED - stating how the "
            f"observation date was obtained, or None; got "
            f"{type(source).__name__} ({source!r})."
        )

    read = _time_dependent_frames_read(settings, frame_path)
    if not read:
        if date is None and source is not None:
            raise ValueError(
                f"A {settings.direction.name} job was given "
                f"observation_date_source={source.name}, but no frame this "
                f"job reads is time-dependent, so it states no observation "
                f"date and there is no date whose source could be recorded. "
                f"A source stated for a job that used no date would put a "
                f"sentence about a date into a record of a conversion that "
                f"never read one. Pass observation_date_source=None."
            )
        if date is not None:
            raise ValueError(
                f"A {settings.direction.name} job was given observation_date="
                f"{date.isoformat()}, but no frame this job reads is "
                f"time-dependent, so no date enters any arithmetic: "
                + (
                    f"its positions are in {frame_path.source.code}, a "
                    f"plate-fixed frame published at its reference epoch."
                    if frame_path is not None
                    else "it reads and writes only State Plane coordinates."
                )
                + " A date stated for a job that used none would put an "
                f"observation epoch into a record of a conversion that never "
                f"read one. Pass observation_date=None."
            )
        return None, ()

    names = " and ".join(frame.code for frame in read)
    if date is None:
        raise ValueError(
            f"This job has positions in {names}, a time-dependent frame, at "
            f"one end, and was given no observation_date. In {names} a Michigan "
            f"point moves "
            f"about 17 mm a year against {NAD83_2011.code}, so the position "
            f"means nothing until the date it was observed is stated, and "
            f"there is no default date to assume. State the calendar date the "
            f"positions were observed (one date for the whole job, "
            f"docs/PLAN-wgs84.md decision D10)."
        )
    running = today()
    if date > running:
        raise ValueError(
            f"observation_date {date.isoformat()} is after the day this job "
            f"runs ({running.isoformat()}). An observation cannot be in the "
            f"future, and HTDP would compute a position for one without "
            f"complaint, moved by the plate motion of time that has not "
            f"passed (docs/PLAN-wgs84.md decision D9). Check the date."
        )
    if source is ObservationDateSource.TODAY:
        local = today_local()
        if date != local:
            raise ValueError(
                f"observation_date_source is TODAY, but observation_date is "
                f"{date.isoformat()} and the day this job runs is "
                f"{local.isoformat()} (the local calendar date). TODAY means "
                f"the date the job ran, so the two must be the same date: a "
                f"date carried over from an earlier session, or a UTC date "
                f"taken for the local one, would be recorded as the date the "
                f"job ran when it is not. State ENTERED for a date that was "
                f"typed, or pass today's local date."
            )
    epoch = htdp.ObservationEpoch(date)

    warnings: list[ConversionWarning] = []
    for frame in read:
        period = realization_period_of_use(frame)
        if period is None or period.contains(date):
            continue
        span = (
            f"from {period.first_day.isoformat()} to "
            f"{period.last_day.isoformat()} (NGA replaced it with "
            f"{period.succeeded_by} on "
            f"{(period.last_day + datetime.timedelta(days=1)).isoformat()})"
            if period.last_day is not None
            else f"from {period.first_day.isoformat()}, with no end yet"
        )
        when = (
            add("")
        if swap_models is not None:
            lines.extend(
                _geoid_swap_elevation_block(
                    result, transformation, swap_models, settings.input_unit
                )
            )
        else:
            lines.extend(_vertical_elevation_block(result, transformation))
    add("")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- warnings
    add(_THIN)
    add("WARNINGS")
    add(_THIN)
    all_warnings = result.warnings
    # Warnings about the JOB, not a point (JobResult.job_warnings): today only
    # the realization period of plan decision D9. JobResult.warnings does not
    # include them, so they are read here, beside it, and printed first,
    # because they are about every point at once. Empty on every same-frame
    # job, whose WARNINGS section is unchanged by a byte.
    job_warnings = result.job_warnings
    if not all_warnings and not job_warnings:
        if zoneless:
            # No zone, so no "inside its zone" to have been.
            add("None. Every point converted cleanly.")
        else:
            add("None. Every point converted cleanly, inside its zone.")
    else:
        if job_warnings:
            add(
                f"{len(all_warnings)} warning(s) across {len(result.points)} "
                f"points, and {len(job_warnings)} about the job as a whole."
            )
        else:
            add(f"{len(all_warnings)} warning(s) across {len(result.points)} points.")
        add("")
        add("A warning is not an error. Every point below was converted, and the")
        add("coordinate written for it is correct. A warning means something about")
        add("the job is worth a second look.")
        job_codes = []
        for warning in job_warnings:
            if warning.code not in job_codes:
                job_codes.append(warning.code)
        for code in job_codes:
            add("")
            add(f"  {_warning_heading(code)} (the whole job)")
            add("")
            for warning in job_warnings:
                if warning.code is code:
                    add(f"    {warning.message}")
        for code in _warning_codes_present(result):
            group = result.warnings_of(code)
            if not group:
                continue
            add("")
            add(f"  {_warning_heading(code)} ({len(group)} point(s))")
            add("")
            for point_id, warning in group[:20]:
                add(f"    {warning.message}")
            if len(group) > 20:
                add(f"    ... and {len(group) - 20} more; see the _full.csv export.")
    add("")

    # -------------------------------------------------------------- files

## Non-negotiable conventions (enforce in review)

- Units explicit at every boundary; International feet is the default because
  Michigan legislated it, and the unit in force is stated in every output file.
- Longitude sign convention: **no default in the core** — `JobSettings`
  requires it and `job.run` refuses a geodetic job without it. The GUI dropdown
  opens on positive-west (DESIGN.md #29, owner's instruction); that is a
  decision, not a regression, and #29 says why.
- Fail closed, never fabricate; refusals name the offending item.
- Missing elevation writes `N/A` in factor columns — never `1.0`.
- One authoritative representation per fact; derived values are never stored.
- One entry point per data path; loaders validate as strictly as the UI.
- No uncited constants — document and page, in an adjacent comment.
- Exports never silently clobber; atomic stage-and-rename.
- No load-bearing asserts in production code; the suite runs under `-O`.
- Cross-frame conversion refuses loudly. Never a silent pass-through.
- The rigorous §3.1 Lambert equations are the only computation path. What
  verifies them is external and lives in the suite: the frozen NGS NCAT anchors
  and the published Appendix C constants (DESIGN.md #14).

## Development process

Work packages by subagents → session lead independently re-derives load-bearing
math before accepting → independent adversarial review (Codex CLI) at the build
midpoint AND at closing → fix at the root, pin each finding with the reviewer's
own counterexample, falsify the pin, then narrowing re-confirmation until
approved. Commit per gated milestone; push after gates. Suite green in both
`pytest` and `-O` modes before any commit.

class ResultsModel(QAbstractTableModel):
    """A read-only table of already-formatted strings.

    Read-only on purpose: core result records are frozen and the interface never
    mutates a computed value (docs/DESIGN.md section 4).
    """

    def __init__(self, parent=None) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._rows: tuple[tuple[str, ...], ...] = ()
        self._warning_messages: tuple[str, ...] = ()
        self._columns: tuple[str, ...] = COLUMNS
        self._warnings_column: int = _warnings_index(COLUMNS)
        self._aligned_right: frozenset[int] = _right_aligned(COLUMNS)

    def set_result(self, result: JobResult | None) -> None:
        self.beginResetModel()
        # The headings are settled here, with the rows, so the two can never
        # describe different jobs: a model reset repaints both together. The
        # warnings and alignment positions are derived from the header at the
        # same moment, because a vertical job's table is two columns wider
        # and a fixed index would paint the wrong cell amber.
        self._columns = columns_for(result)
        self._warnings_column = _warnings_index(self._columns)
        self._aligned_right = _right_aligned(self._columns)
        if result is None:
            self._rows = ()
            self._warning_messages = ()
        else:
            self._rows = row_strings(result)
            self._warning_messages = tuple(
                "\n\n".join(w.message for w in point.warnings) for point in result.points
            )
        self.endResetModel()

    def rowCount(self, parent=QModelIndex()) -> int:
        if parent.isValid():
            return 0
        return len(self._rows)

    def columnCount(self, parent=QModelIndex()) -> int:
        if parent.isValid():
            return 0
        return len(self._columns)

    def data(self, index, role=Qt.ItemDataRole.DisplayRole):
        if not index.isValid():
            return None
        row = index.row()
        column = index.column()
        if row < 0 or row >= len(self._rows):
            return None
        if column < 0 or column >= len(self._columns):
            return None


def _visible_sequence(combo) -> list:
    return [
        combo.itemData(i) for i in range(combo.count()) if not combo.view().isRowHidden(i)
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "shown_keys",
    [
        None,  # the defaults
        "all",
        "only-83-and-nad",
        "only-2022-and-wgs",
    ],
)
def test_no_two_rules_stand_together_and_none_ends_the_list(qapp, shown_keys):
    """A separator is shown only BETWEEN two shown entries: never first after
    the placeholder, never last, never two together - whatever is switched
    off (``options.visible_rows``)."""
    keys = {
        None: options.DEFAULT_SHOWN,
        "all": frozenset(g.key for g in options.LIST_GROUPS),
        "only-83-and-nad": frozenset(
            {"lists/zones/SPCS 83", "lists/geodetic/NAD83(2011)"}
        ),
        "only-2022-and-wgs": frozenset(
            {"lists/zones/SPCS2022", "lists/geodetic/WGS84(G2296)"}
        ),
    }[shown_keys]
    win = MainWindow(store=settings_store.MemoryStore())
    try:
        win.apply_options(replaced(options.DEFAULT_OPTIONS, shown=keys))
        for combo in (win.from_zone, win.to_zone, win.single_point.to_zone):
            sequence = _visible_sequence(combo)
            assert sequence[0] == controls.UNCHOSEN
            assert sequence[1] is not None
            assert sequence[-1] is not None
            for before, after in zip(sequence, sequence[1:]):
                assert not (before is None and after is None)
    finally:
        win.close()


def test_the_default_lists_read_as_the_owner_will_see_them(window):
    """The whole visible TO list under the defaults, written out: one rule
    between the positions and Auto, one between Auto and the 1983 zones, one
    before the 2022 zones. On a FROM list there is no Auto row and one rule
    between the positions and the zones."""
    from michspc.spc.zones import SPCS2022_ZONES

    assert _visible_sequence(window.to_zone) == [
        controls.UNCHOSEN, NAD, NAT, None, AUTO_NAD, None,
        *SPCS83_ZONES, None, *SPCS2022_ZONES,
    ]
    assert _visible_sequence(window.from_zone) == [
        controls.UNCHOSEN, NAD, NAT, None, *SPCS83_ZONES, None, *SPCS2022_ZONES,
    ]

+"""Exercise Qt-independent GUI rules from their source AST, without Qt widgets.
+
+This does not simulate or claim verification of Qt event delivery.
+"""
+import ast, sys, types, itertools, datetime
+from pathlib import Path
+from dataclasses import replace
+
+def isolated(name,path,exclude_classes=(),exclude_assignments=()):
+    tree=ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf8'),path)
+    tree.body=[n for n in tree.body if not (
+        isinstance(n,(ast.Import,ast.ImportFrom)) and getattr(n,'module','') and n.module.startswith('PySide6')
+        or isinstance(n,ast.ClassDef) and n.name in exclude_classes
+        or isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t,ast.Name) and t.id in exclude_assignments for t in n.targets)
+    )]
+    module=types.ModuleType(name)
+    module.__file__=path
+    sys.modules[name]=module
+    exec(compile(tree,path,'exec'),module.__dict__)
+    return module
+
+c=isolated('michspc.gui.controls','michspc/gui/controls.py',('_CalendarOpensOnToday','ObservationDateEdit'),('OBSERVATION_DATE_UNANSWERED',))

+ss.set_store_factory(ss.MemoryStore)

+from michspc.fileio import pnezd
+from michspc.spc.frames import NAD83_2011,NATRF2022,WGS84_G1762,WGS84_G2139,WGS84_G2296,ITRF2014,ITRF2020

+    result=job.run(settings,pnezd.parse_lines([f'P,{a.latitude},{a.longitude},,probe\n']))
+    sections=r.single_point_sections(result)

+    point=result.points[0]
+    assert a.expected in text
+    assert point.auto_zone.county_name in text and point.auto_zone.county_fips in text
+    columns=r.columns_for(result)
+    row=r.row_strings(result)[0]
+    assert len(columns)==len(row)
+    assert row[columns.index('Zone')]==point.auto_zone.zone.name
+    assert row[columns.index('County')]==point.auto_zone.county_name
+    rendered+=1
+
+groups=o.LIST_GROUPS
+items=(c.UNCHOSEN,*c.geodetic_choice_groups()[0],None,*c.geodetic_choice_groups()[1],None,*c.AUTO_CHOICES,None,*SPCS83_ZONES,None,*SPCS2022_ZONES)
+combinations=0
+for bits in itertools.product((False,True),repeat=len(groups)):
+    shown=frozenset(g.key for g,b in zip(groups,bits) if b)
+    if o.options_problem(shown): continue
+    for frame,_ in job.AUTO_FRAMES:
+      for source in job.ObservationDateSource:
+        opt=o.Options(shown,frame,source)
+        store=ss.MemoryStore()
+        o.save_options(store,opt)
+        assert o.load_options(store)==(opt,())
+        assert set(store.values)=={*(g.key for g in groups),o.AUTO_FRAME_KEY,o.DATE_SOURCE_KEY}
+        assert all(not isinstance(v,datetime.date) for v in store.values.values())
+        for is_source in [False,True]:
+            flags=o.visible_rows(opt,items,is_source=is_source)
+            seq=[v for v,f in zip(items,flags) if f]
+            assert seq[0]==c.UNCHOSEN and seq[1] is not None and seq[-1] is not None
+            assert not any(a is None and b is None for a,b in zip(seq,seq[1:]))
+            assert not any(c.is_auto(v) for v in seq) if is_source else True
+        combinations+=1
+
+invalids=[True,False,1,0,1.0,[],{},'True','False','1','0','yesterday','NAD83',b'true']
+checked=0
+for key in [*(g.key for g in groups),o.AUTO_FRAME_KEY,o.DATE_SOURCE_KEY]:
+  for value in invalids:
+    store=ss.MemoryStore({key:value})
+    value1,messages=o.load_options(store)
+    assert value1==o.DEFAULT_OPTIONS,(key,value)
+    assert messages,(key,value)
+    assert o.load_options(store)==(o.DEFAULT_OPTIONS,())
+    checked+=1
+assert US_SURVEY_FEET not in c.auto_units(c.auto_choice(NATRF2022))
+assert ss.PRODUCTION_STORES_BUILT==0
+print('pure renderers:',rendered,'anchors; Options:',combinations,'round trips; invalid persisted values:',checked,'PASS')

def test_each_clean_file_is_described_with_its_own_zone_and_count(tmp_path):
    _, archive = auto_job(tmp_path, TAGS_83, "SPCS 83", NAD83_2011)
    record = members_of(archive)["pts_AUTO-SPCS83_README.txt"]
    flat = " ".join(record.split())
    assert (
        "pts_AUTO-SPCS83_MI-N.csv The 1 point placed in Michigan North, zone 2111 "
        "(MI-N): its converted coordinates in that zone"
    ) in flat
    assert (
        "pts_AUTO-SPCS83_MI-C.csv The 2 points placed in Michigan Central, zone "
        "2112 (MI-C), in input file order: their converted coordinates"
    ) in flat
    assert "Import it into CAD as Michigan South (2113) coordinates." in flat


FALSE_ON_AUTO = (
    # Each sentence below names one zone, one target, a zone-to-zone move or
    # the fixed-zone file count; each is false on an Auto job.
    "Both zones are on the same reference frame",
    "moved between zones",
    "both zones' coordinates",
    "both convergence angles",
    "It contains the three files below" + "\n" + "purpose. A coordinate file",
    "Extract this one and import it into CAD",
    "AUTO_ZONE",
    "AutoZone",
    "TO: Michigan",
    "OUTSIDE THE TARGET ZONE",
)


def _sweep_jobs():
    for tags, system, frame in (
        (TAGS_83, "SPCS 83", NAD83_2011),
        (TAGS_2022, "SPCS2022", NATRF2022),
    ):
        yield f"{system}-mixed", tags, system, frame, {}
        yield f"{system}-single", tags[:1], system, frame, {}
    for tags in (TAGS_83, TAGS_83[:1]):
        yield (
            f"wgs84-{len(tags)}",
            tags,
            "SPCS 83",
            NAD83_2011,
            dict(geodetic_frame=WGS84_G2296, observation_date=datetime.date(2025, 6, 15)),
        )
    yield "hv", TAGS_83, "SPCS 83", NAD83_2011, dict(
        vertical_mode=VerticalMode.HORIZONTAL_AND_VERTICAL,
        source_vertical_datum=NGVD29,
        target_vertical_datum=NAVD88,
    )
    yield "ellipsoid", TAGS_83, "SPCS 83", NAD83_2011, dict(
        input_height_kind=HeightKind.ELLIPSOID
    )
    yield "no-geoid", TAGS_2022, "SPCS2022", NATRF2022, dict(geoid_model=None)
    yield "itrf-hv-ellipsoid", TAGS_83, "SPCS 83", NAD83_2011, dict(
        geodetic_frame=ITRF2020,
        observation_date=datetime.date(2024, 1, 1),
        vertical_mode=VerticalMode.HORIZONTAL_AND_VERTICAL,
        source_vertical_datum=NAVD88,
        target_vertical_datum=NAVD88,
        input_height_kind=HeightKind.ELLIPSOID,
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("label, tags, system, frame, fields", list(_sweep_jobs()), ids=lambda v: v if isinstance(v, str) and "-" in v or v in ("hv", "ellipsoid") else "")
def test_no_one_zone_sentence_survives_on_an_auto_job(tmp_path, label, tags, system, frame, fields):
    fields = dict(fields)
    geodetic = fields.pop("geodetic_frame", frame)
    path = write_input(tmp_path, tags, system)
    result = run(auto_settings(path, frame, geodetic_frame=geodetic, **fields))
    files = members_of(exports.write_all(result)["archive"])
    (record,) = [text for name, text in files.items() if name.endswith("_README.txt")]
    flat = " ".join(record.split())
    for sentence in FALSE_ON_AUTO:
        assert " ".join(sentence.split()) not in flat, (label, sentence)
    assert "TO: Auto zone - each point in the zone of its county" in record
    if geodetic is not frame:
        assert f"Position tested    Each point's {frame.code} position, as the frame" in record
        assert "FRAME TRANSFORMATION" in record
    else:
        assert "Position tested" not in record
    # Every clean file's points are the job's points, each once.
    seen = []
    for name, text in files.items():
        if not name.endswith(("_full.csv", "_README.txt")):
            seen.extend(line.split(",")[0] for line in text.strip().split("\r\n"))
    assert sorted(seen, key=int) == [str(i + 1) for i in range(len(tags))]


def test_the_auto_scale_factor_sentence(tmp_path):
    _, archive = auto_job(tmp_path, TAGS_83, "SPCS 83", NAD83_2011)
    record = members_of(archive)["pts_AUTO-SPCS83_README.txt"]
    assert (
        "Each point's grid scale factor and convergence are those of its own "
        "zone, named in its row's Target zone cell of the _full.csv export. The "
        "summary below is across all 5 point(s), in the 3 zones this job used."
    ) in " ".join(record.split())


def test_a_wgs84_auto_job_writes_the_fixed_zone_jobs_lines(tmp_path):
    """WGS 84 in, Auto on NAD83(2011): the zone is chosen at the pivot, and
    each clean file is the fixed-zone WGS 84 job's lines for its points."""
    path = write_input(tmp_path, TAGS_83, "SPCS 83")
    settings = auto_settings(
        path, NAD83_2011, geodetic_frame=WGS84_G2296, observation_date=datetime.date(2025, 6, 15)
    )
    result = run(settings)
    files = members_of(exports.write_all(result)["archive"])
    for zone in (MI_NORTH, MI_CENTRAL, MI_SOUTH):
        out = tmp_path / zone.abbrev
        out.mkdir()
        fixed = run(dataclasses.replace(settings, target_zone=zone, auto_frame=None, output_directory=out))
        fixed_lines = members_of(exports.write_all(fixed)["archive"])[f"pts_{zone.abbrev}.csv"].split("\r\n")
        ids = {p.point_id for p in result.points if p.auto_zone.zone is zone}
        expected = [line for line in fixed_lines if line.split(",")[0] in ids]
        assert files[f"pts_AUTO-SPCS83_{zone.abbrev}.csv"] == "\r\n".join(expected) + "\r\n"
    audit = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(files["pts_AUTO-SPCS83_full.csv"])))
    assert audit[0][-1].startswith("Velocity up")
    assert audit[0].index("County") == audit[0].index("Target zone") + 1

def _dated_record(tmp_path, source, auto=False):
    out = tmp_path / f"{source}-{auto}"
    out.mkdir()
    path = write_input(out, TAGS_83[:2], "SPCS 83")
    fields = dict(
        geodetic_frame=WGS84_G2296,
        observation_date=LOCAL,
        observation_date_source=source,
    )
    if not auto:
        fields.update(target_zone=MI_SOUTH, auto_frame=None)
    result = run(auto_settings(path, NAD83_2011, **fields))
    files = members_of(exports.write_all(result)["archive"])
    (record,) = [t for n, t in files.items() if n.endswith("_README.txt")]
    (audit,) = [t for n, t in files.items() if n.endswith("_full.csv")]
    return record.split("\n", 6)[6].replace(str(out), "<ROOT>"), audit


def test_the_frozen_list_covers_every_member_of_every_configuration():
    """Anti-vacuousness: a fixture that lost half its lines would let half the
    outputs drift while the comparison below still passed."""
    frozen = _frozen()
    assert len(frozen) == 18
    for name, _configuration in _configurations():
        assert any(member.startswith(f"{name}/") for member in frozen)


def test_no_default_job_writes_a_different_byte_than_v0_5_0(tmp_path):
    """The regression floor, against digests v0.5.0 itself computed."""
    written = _written_digests(tmp_path)
    frozen = _frozen()

    assert sorted(written) == sorted(frozen)
    differing = {
        member: (frozen[member], written[member])
        for member in frozen
        if frozen[member] != written[member]
    }
    assert not differing, (
        "these members differ from what v0.5.0 wrote for the same job: "
        + ", ".join(sorted(differing))
    )

+"""Exercise Qt-independent GUI rules from their source AST, without Qt widgets.
+
+This does not simulate or claim verification of Qt event delivery.
+"""
+import ast, sys, types, itertools, datetime
+from pathlib import Path
+from dataclasses import replace
+
+def isolated(name,path,exclude_classes=(),exclude_assignments=()):
+    tree=ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf8'),path)
+    tree.body=[n for n in tree.body if not (
+        isinstance(n,(ast.Import,ast.ImportFrom)) and getattr(n,'module','') and n.module.startswith('PySide6')
+        or isinstance(n,ast.ClassDef) and n.name in exclude_classes
+        or isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t,ast.Name) and t.id in exclude_assignments for t in n.targets)
+    )]
+    module=types.ModuleType(name)
+    module.__file__=path
+    sys.modules[name]=module
+    exec(compile(tree,path,'exec'),module.__dict__)
+    return module
+
+c=isolated('michspc.gui.controls','michspc/gui/controls.py',('_CalendarOpensOnToday','ObservationDateEdit'),('OBSERVATION_DATE_UNANSWERED',))
+from michspc.gui import options as o, settings_store as ss
+ss.set_store_factory(ss.MemoryStore)
+r=isolated('michspc.gui.results_model','michspc/gui/results_model.py',('ResultsModel',),('AMBER',))
+from michspc import job
+from michspc.fileio import pnezd
+from michspc.spc.frames import NAD83_2011,NATRF2022,WGS84_G1762,WGS84_G2139,WGS84_G2296,ITRF2014,ITRF2020
+from michspc.spc.zones import SPCS83_ZONES,SPCS2022_ZONES
+from michspc.spc.units import METERS,US_SURVEY_FEET
+from tests.fixtures.autozone_anchors import ANCHORS
+
+rendered=0
+for a in ANCHORS:
+    if not a.expected: continue
+    frame=NAD83_2011 if a.system=='SPCS 83' else NATRF2022
+    settings=job.JobSettings(input_path=None,output_directory=None,direction=job.Direction.GEODETIC_TO_ZONE,source_zone=None,target_zone=job.AUTO_ZONE,input_unit=METERS,output_unit=METERS,longitude_convention=job.LongitudeConvention.NEGATIVE_WEST,geodetic_frame=frame,auto_frame=frame)
+    result=job.run(settings,pnezd.parse_lines([f'P,{a.latitude},{a.longitude},,probe\n']))
+    sections=r.single_point_sections(result)
+    text=r.single_point_clipboard_text(sections)
+    point=result.points[0]
+    assert a.expected in text
+    assert point.auto_zone.county_name in text and point.auto_zone.county_fips in text
+    columns=r.columns_for(result)
+    row=r.row_strings(result)[0]
+    assert len(columns)==len(row)
+    assert row[columns.index('Zone')]==point.auto_zone.zone.name
+    assert row[columns.index('County')]==point.auto_zone.county_name
+    rendered+=1
+
+groups=o.LIST_GROUPS
+items=(c.UNCHOSEN,*c.geodetic_choice_groups()[0],None,*c.geodetic_choice_groups()[1],None,*c.AUTO_CHOICES,None,*SPCS83_ZONES,None,*SPCS2022_ZONES)
+combinations=0
+for bits in itertools.product((False,True),repeat=len(groups)):
+    shown=frozenset(g.key for g,b in zip(groups,bits) if b)
+    if o.options_problem(shown): continue
+    for frame,_ in job.AUTO_FRAMES:
+      for source in job.ObservationDateSource:
+        opt=o.Options(shown,frame,source)
+        store=ss.MemoryStore()
+        o.save_options(store,opt)
+        assert o.load_options(store)==(opt,())
+        assert set(store.values)=={*(g.key for g in groups),o.AUTO_FRAME_KEY,o.DATE_SOURCE_KEY}
+        assert all(not isinstance(v,datetime.date) for v in store.values.values())
+        for is_source in [False,True]:
+            flags=o.visible_rows(opt,items,is_source=is_source)
+            seq=[v for v,f in zip(items,flags) if f]
+            assert seq[0]==c.UNCHOSEN and seq[1] is not None and seq[-1] is not None
+            assert not any(a is None and b is None for a,b in zip(seq,seq[1:]))
+            assert not any(c.is_auto(v) for v in seq) if is_source else True
+        combinations+=1
+
+invalids=[True,False,1,0,1.0,[],{},'True','False','1','0','yesterday','NAD83',b'true']
+checked=0
+for key in [*(g.key for g in groups),o.AUTO_FRAME_KEY,o.DATE_SOURCE_KEY]:
+  for value in invalids:
+    store=ss.MemoryStore({key:value})
+    value1,messages=o.load_options(store)
+    assert value1==o.DEFAULT_OPTIONS,(key,value)
+    assert messages,(key,value)
+    assert o.load_options(store)==(o.DEFAULT_OPTIONS,())
+    checked+=1
+assert US_SURVEY_FEET not in c.auto_units(c.auto_choice(NATRF2022))
+assert ss.PRODUCTION_STORES_BUILT==0
+print('pure renderers:',rendered,'anchors; Options:',combinations,'round trips; invalid persisted values:',checked,'PASS')

+"""Invoke specific existing digest checks directly; this is not a pytest run."""
+import sys, types
+from pathlib import Path
+
+class Mark:
+    def __getattr__(self,name):
+        return lambda *a,**kw: lambda f:f
+def fixture(f=None,**kw): return f if f is not None else lambda f:f
+p=types.ModuleType('pytest')
+p.mark=Mark()
+p.fixture=fixture
+p.param=lambda *a,**kw: a
+sys.modules['pytest']=p
+from tests import test_orthometric_regression as orth
+from tests import test_same_frame_outputs_unchanged as same
+from tests import test_frame_disclosure as frame
+root=Path('reviewer_scratch/digest').resolve()
+root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+for name,f in [('orthometric',orth.test_no_default_job_writes_a_different_byte_than_v0_5_0),('same_frame',same.test_no_same_frame_job_writes_a_different_byte_than_before_w4),('frame',frame.test_g2g_reading_no_geoid_is_byte_identical_to_w4)]:
+    d=root/name
+    d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    f(d)
+    print(name,'frozen digest comparison PASS')

+from pathlib import Path
+from dataclasses import replace
+from michspc import job,selftest
+from michspc.fileio import counties
+from tools.build_release import foreign_bundle_files
+
+root=Path('reviewer_scratch/seeds').resolve()
+root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+
+def fails(label,action):
+    try: action()
+    except selftest.SelfTestError as error: print(label,'CAUGHT:',str(error).splitlines()[0][:230])
+    else: raise RuntimeError(label+' incorrectly passed')
+
+original_project=job.project_point
+try:
+    # Auto and fixed agree with each other on raw latitude/longitude instead
+    # of a projection. Only NCAT's independent values can catch this.
+    job.project_point=lambda lat,lon,*a,**kw: replace(original_project(lat,lon,*a,**kw),target_northing=lat,target_easting=lon)
+    fails('no-op projection',selftest.check_autozone)
+finally: job.project_point=original_project
+
+original_data=counties.DATA_DIR
+try:
+    counties.DATA_DIR=root
+    counties.default_counties.cache_clear()
+    fails('missing county file',selftest.check_autozone)
+    raw=(original_data/counties.COUNTIES_FILENAME).read_bytes()
+    (root/counties.COUNTIES_FILENAME).write_bytes(raw.replace(b'Calhoun',b'Wrong!!'))
+    counties.default_counties.cache_clear()
+    fails('wrong county file',selftest.check_autozone)
+finally:
+    counties.DATA_DIR=original_data
+    counties.default_counties.cache_clear()
+
+bundle=root/'bundle'
+review=root/'review'
+(bundle/'_internal/data').mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
+review.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+(review/'capture.txt').write_bytes(b'reviewer-private-capture\n')
+(bundle/'_internal/data/innocent.bin').write_bytes(b'reviewer-private-capture\n')
+found=foreign_bundle_files(bundle,review)
+if len(found)!=1 or 'innocent.bin' not in found[0]: raise RuntimeError(found)
+print('gate 6 renamed review capture: CAUGHT',found)
+print('restored selftest14:',selftest.check_autozone())

+from pathlib import Path
+from dataclasses import replace
+import csv, datetime, io, zipfile, json
+from michspc import job, selftest
+from michspc.fileio import exports, pnezd
+from michspc.spc.frames import NAD83_2011, NATRF2022, WGS84_G1762, WGS84_G2139, WGS84_G2296, ITRF2014, ITRF2020
+from michspc.spc.units import METERS, INTERNATIONAL_FEET, US_SURVEY_FEET
+from michspc.spc.zones import zone_by_code
+from michspc.spc.convert import WarningCode
+from tests.fixtures.autozone_anchors import ANCHORS
+
+root=Path('reviewer_scratch').resolve()
+root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+counts=dict(anchors=0, refused=0, auto_archives=0, fixed_archives=0, compared_points=0)
+for system,frame in [('SPCS 83',NAD83_2011),('SPCS2022',NATRF2022)]:
+    anchors=[a for a in ANCHORS if a.system==system]
+    inside=[a for a in anchors if a.expected]
+    for a in anchors:
+        settings=job.JobSettings(input_path=None,output_directory=None,direction=job.Direction.GEODETIC_TO_ZONE,source_zone=None,target_zone=job.AUTO_ZONE,input_unit=METERS,output_unit=METERS,longitude_convention=job.LongitudeConvention.NEGATIVE_WEST,geodetic_frame=frame,auto_frame=frame)
+        parsed=pnezd.parse_lines([f'A,{a.latitude},{a.longitude},,probe\n'])
+        try: result=job.run(settings,parsed)
+        except job.AutoZoneUnplacedPointsError:
+            assert a.expected is None,a
+            counts['refused']+=1
+        else:
+            assert result.points[0].auto_zone.zone.code==a.expected,a
+        counts['anchors']+=1
+    input_frames=[frame] if frame is NATRF2022 else [frame,WGS84_G1762,WGS84_G2139,WGS84_G2296,ITRF2014,ITRF2020]
+    for input_frame in input_frames:
+      for unit in [METERS,INTERNATIONAL_FEET]+([US_SURVEY_FEET] if frame is NAD83_2011 else []):
+        dest=root/f'{system.replace(" ","")}_{input_frame.code.replace("/","_")}_{unit.code}'
+        dest.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+        path=dest/'points.csv'
+        path.write_text(''.join(f'{i},{a.latitude},{a.longitude},{200 if i%2 else ""},{a.tag}\n' for i,a in enumerate(inside)),encoding='utf8')
+        settings=job.JobSettings(input_path=path,output_directory=dest,direction=job.Direction.GEODETIC_TO_ZONE,source_zone=None,target_zone=job.AUTO_ZONE,input_unit=METERS,output_unit=unit,longitude_convention=job.LongitudeConvention.NEGATIVE_WEST,geodetic_frame=input_frame,auto_frame=frame,**(dict(observation_date=datetime.date(2025,6,15),observation_date_source=job.ObservationDateSource.ENTERED) if input_frame.is_time_dependent else {}))
+        result=job.run(settings)
+        archive=exports.write_all(result,overwrite=True)['archive']
+        counts['auto_archives']+=1
+        with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as z:
+            files={n:z.read(n) for n in z.namelist()}
+        used={a.expected for a in inside}
+        assert len(files)==len(used)+2
+        for code in used:
+            zone=zone_by_code(code)
+            fixed=job.run(replace(settings,target_zone=zone,auto_frame=None))
+            fa=exports.write_all(fixed,overwrite=True)['archive']
+            counts['fixed_archives']+=1
+            ids={str(i) for i,a in enumerate(inside) if a.expected==code}
+            with zipfile.ZipFile(fa) as z: fixed_rows=list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(z.read(f'points_{zone.abbrev}.csv').decode())))
+            expected=[r for r in fixed_rows if r[0] in ids]
+            actual=list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(files[f'{exports.output_stem(result)}_{zone.abbrev}.csv'].decode())))
+            assert actual==expected,(system,input_frame,unit,code)
+            for ap,fp,a in zip(result.points,fixed.points,inside):
+                if a.expected!=code: continue
+                assert ap.auto_zone.zone.code==code
+                assert repr((ap.output_northing,ap.output_easting,ap.output_elevation,ap.factors))==repr((fp.output_northing,fp.output_easting,fp.output_elevation,fp.factors))
+                assert not any(w.code is WarningCode.OUTSIDE_ZONE_EXTENT for w in ap.warnings+ap.conversion.warnings)
+                counts['compared_points']+=1
+        record=files[f'{exports.output_stem(result)}_README.txt'].decode()
+        if frame is NATRF2022: assert 'MCL 54.234' not in record
+        try: exports.clean_pnezd_rows(result)
+        except Exception: pass
+        else: raise AssertionError('cross-zone renderer accepted')
+print(json.dumps(counts,sort_keys=True))
+print('selftest14:',selftest.check_autozone())

+"""Exercise Qt-independent GUI rules from their source AST, without Qt widgets.
+
+This does not simulate or claim verification of Qt event delivery.
+"""
+import ast, sys, types, itertools, datetime
+from pathlib import Path
+from dataclasses import replace
+
+def isolated(name,path,exclude_classes=(),exclude_assignments=()):
+    tree=ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf8'),path)
+    tree.body=[n for n in tree.body if not (
+        isinstance(n,(ast.Import,ast.ImportFrom)) and getattr(n,'module','') and n.module.startswith('PySide6')
+        or isinstance(n,ast.ClassDef) and n.name in exclude_classes
+        or isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t,ast.Name) and t.id in exclude_assignments for t in n.targets)
+    )]
+    module=types.ModuleType(name)
+    module.__file__=path
+    sys.modules[name]=module
+    exec(compile(tree,path,'exec'),module.__dict__)
+    return module
+
+c=isolated('michspc.gui.controls','michspc/gui/controls.py',('_CalendarOpensOnToday','ObservationDateEdit'),('OBSERVATION_DATE_UNANSWERED',))
+from michspc.gui import options as o, settings_store as ss
+ss.set_store_factory(ss.MemoryStore)
+r=isolated('michspc.gui.results_model','michspc/gui/results_model.py',('ResultsModel',),('AMBER',))
+from michspc import job
+from michspc.fileio import pnezd
+from michspc.spc.frames import NAD83_2011,NATRF2022,WGS84_G1762,WGS84_G2139,WGS84_G2296,ITRF2014,ITRF2020
+from michspc.spc.zones import SPCS83_ZONES,SPCS2022_ZONES
+from michspc.spc.units import METERS,US_SURVEY_FEET
+from tests.fixtures.autozone_anchors import ANCHORS
+
+rendered=0
+for a in ANCHORS:
+    if not a.expected: continue
+    frame=NAD83_2011 if a.system=='SPCS 83' else NATRF2022
+    settings=job.JobSettings(input_path=None,output_directory=None,direction=job.Direction.GEODETIC_TO_ZONE,source_zone=None,target_zone=job.AUTO_ZONE,input_unit=METERS,output_unit=METERS,longitude_convention=job.LongitudeConvention.NEGATIVE_WEST,geodetic_frame=frame,auto_frame=frame)
+    result=job.run(settings,pnezd.parse_lines([f'P,{a.latitude},{a.longitude},,probe\n']))
+    sections=r.single_point_sections(result)
+    text=r.single_point_clipboard_text(sections)
+    point=result.points[0]
+    assert a.expected in text
+    assert point.auto_zone.county_name in text and point.auto_zone.county_fips in text
+    columns=r.columns_for(result)
+    row=r.row_strings(result)[0]
+    assert len(columns)==len(row)
+    assert row[columns.index('Zone')]==point.auto_zone.zone.name
+    assert row[columns.index('County')]==point.auto_zone.county_name
+    rendered+=1
+
+groups=o.LIST_GROUPS
+items=(c.UNCHOSEN,*c.geodetic_choice_groups()[0],None,*c.geodetic_choice_groups()[1],None,*c.AUTO_CHOICES,None,*SPCS83_ZONES,None,*SPCS2022_ZONES)
+combinations=0
+for bits in itertools.product((False,True),repeat=len(groups)):
+    shown=frozenset(g.key for g,b in zip(groups,bits) if b)
+    if o.options_problem(shown): continue
+    for frame,_ in job.AUTO_FRAMES:
+      for source in job.ObservationDateSource:
+        opt=o.Options(shown,frame,source)
+        store=ss.MemoryStore()
+        o.save_options(store,opt)
+        assert o.load_options(store)==(opt,())
+        assert set(store.values)=={*(g.key for g in groups),o.AUTO_FRAME_KEY,o.DATE_SOURCE_KEY}
+        assert all(not isinstance(v,datetime.date) for v in store.values.values())
+        for is_source in [False,True]:
+            flags=o.visible_rows(opt,items,is_source=is_source)
+            seq=[v for v,f in zip(items,flags) if f]
+            assert seq[0]==c.UNCHOSEN and seq[1] is not None and seq[-1] is not None
+            assert not any(a is None and b is None for a,b in zip(seq,seq[1:]))
+            assert not any(c.is_auto(v) for v in seq) if is_source else True
+        combinations+=1
+
+invalids=[True,False,1,0,1.0,[],{},'True','False','1','0','yesterday','NAD83',b'true']
+checked=0
+for key in [*(g.key for g in groups),o.AUTO_FRAME_KEY,o.DATE_SOURCE_KEY]:
+  for value in invalids:
+    store=ss.MemoryStore({key:value})
+    value1,messages=o.load_options(store)
+    assert value1==o.DEFAULT_OPTIONS,(key,value)
+    assert messages,(key,value)
+    assert o.load_options(store)==(o.DEFAULT_OPTIONS,())
+    checked+=1
+assert US_SURVEY_FEET not in c.auto_units(c.auto_choice(NATRF2022))
+assert ss.PRODUCTION_STORES_BUILT==0

+from pathlib import Path
+from dataclasses import replace
+from michspc import job,selftest
+from michspc.fileio import counties
+from tools.build_release import foreign_bundle_files
+
+root=Path('reviewer_scratch/seeds').resolve()
+root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+
+def fails(label,action):
+    try: action()
+    except selftest.SelfTestError as error: print(label,'CAUGHT:',str(error).splitlines()[0][:230])
+    else: raise RuntimeError(label+' incorrectly passed')
+
+original_project=job.project_point
+try:
+    # Auto and fixed agree with each other on raw latitude/longitude instead
+    # of a projection. Only NCAT's independent values can catch this.
+    job.project_point=lambda lat,lon,*a,**kw: replace(original_project(lat,lon,*a,**kw),target_northing=lat,target_easting=lon)

+    (root/counties.COUNTIES_FILENAME).unlink(missing_ok=True)
+    counties.default_counties.cache_clear()
+    fails('missing county file',selftest.check_autozone)
+    raw=(original_data/counties.COUNTIES_FILENAME).read_bytes()
+    (root/counties.COUNTIES_FILENAME).write_bytes(raw.replace(b'Calhoun',b'Wrong!!'))
+    counties.default_counties.cache_clear()
+    fails('wrong county file',selftest.check_autozone)
+finally:
+    counties.DATA_DIR=original_data
+    counties.default_counties.cache_clear()

+(bundle/'_internal/data').mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)

+from pathlib import Path
+from dataclasses import replace
+import csv, datetime, io, zipfile, json
+from michspc import job, selftest
+from michspc.fileio import exports, pnezd
+from michspc.spc.frames import NAD83_2011, NATRF2022, WGS84_G1762, WGS84_G2139, WGS84_G2296, ITRF2014, ITRF2020
+from michspc.spc.units import METERS, INTERNATIONAL_FEET, US_SURVEY_FEET
+from michspc.spc.zones import zone_by_code
+from michspc.spc.convert import WarningCode
+from tests.fixtures.autozone_anchors import ANCHORS
+
+root=Path('reviewer_scratch').resolve()
+root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+counts=dict(anchors=0, refused=0, auto_archives=0, fixed_archives=0, compared_points=0)
+for system,frame in [('SPCS 83',NAD83_2011),('SPCS2022',NATRF2022)]:
+    anchors=[a for a in ANCHORS if a.system==system]
+    inside=[a for a in anchors if a.expected]
+    for a in anchors:
+        settings=job.JobSettings(input_path=None,output_directory=None,direction=job.Direction.GEODETIC_TO_ZONE,source_zone=None,target_zone=job.AUTO_ZONE,input_unit=METERS,output_unit=METERS,longitude_convention=job.LongitudeConvention.NEGATIVE_WEST,geodetic_frame=frame,auto_frame=frame)
+        parsed=pnezd.parse_lines([f'A,{a.latitude},{a.longitude},,probe\n'])
+        try: result=job.run(settings,parsed)
+        except job.AutoZoneUnplacedPointsError:
+            assert a.expected is None,a
+            counts['refused']+=1
+        else:
+            assert result.points[0].auto_zone.zone.code==a.expected,a
+        counts['anchors']+=1
+    input_frames=[frame] if frame is NATRF2022 else [frame,WGS84_G1762,WGS84_G2139,WGS84_G2296,ITRF2014,ITRF2020]
+    for input_frame in input_frames:
+      for unit in [METERS,INTERNATIONAL_FEET]+([US_SURVEY_FEET] if frame is NAD83_2011 else []):
+        dest=root/f'{system.replace(" ","")}_{input_frame.code.replace("/","_")}_{unit.code}'
+        dest.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+        path=dest/'points.csv'
+        path.write_text(''.join(f'{i},{a.latitude},{a.longitude},{200 if i%2 else ""},{a.tag}\n' for i,a in enumerate(inside)),encoding='utf8')
+        settings=job.JobSettings(input_path=path,output_directory=dest,direction=job.Direction.GEODETIC_TO_ZONE,source_zone=None,target_zone=job.AUTO_ZONE,input_unit=METERS,output_unit=unit,longitude_convention=job.LongitudeConvention.NEGATIVE_WEST,geodetic_frame=input_frame,auto_frame=frame,**(dict(observation_date=datetime.date(2025,6,15),observation_date_source=job.ObservationDateSource.ENTERED) if input_frame.is_time_dependent else {}))
+        result=job.run(settings)
+        archive=exports.write_all(result,overwrite=True)['archive']
+        counts['auto_archives']+=1
+        with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as z:
+            files={n:z.read(n) for n in z.namelist()}
+        used={a.expected for a in inside}
+        assert len(files)==len(used)+2
+        for code in used:
+            zone=zone_by_code(code)
+            fixed=job.run(replace(settings,target_zone=zone,auto_frame=None))
+            fa=exports.write_all(fixed,overwrite=True)['archive']
+            counts['fixed_archives']+=1
+            ids={str(i) for i,a in enumerate(inside) if a.expected==code}
+            with zipfile.ZipFile(fa) as z: fixed_rows=list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(z.read(f'points_{zone.abbrev}.csv').decode())))
+            expected=[r for r in fixed_rows if r[0] in ids]
+            actual=list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(files[f'{exports.output_stem(result)}_{zone.abbrev}.csv'].decode())))
+            assert actual==expected,(system,input_frame,unit,code)
+            for ap,fp,a in zip(result.points,fixed.points,inside):
+                if a.expected!=code: continue
+                assert ap.auto_zone.zone.code==code
+                assert repr((ap.output_northing,ap.output_easting,ap.output_elevation,ap.factors))==repr((fp.output_northing,fp.output_easting,fp.output_elevation,fp.factors))
+                assert not any(w.code is WarningCode.OUTSIDE_ZONE_EXTENT for w in ap.warnings+ap.conversion.warnings)
+                counts['compared_points']+=1
+        record=files[f'{exports.output_stem(result)}_README.txt'].decode()
+        if frame is NATRF2022: assert 'MCL 54.234' not in record
+        try: exports.clean_pnezd_rows(result)
+        except Exception: pass
+        else: raise AssertionError('cross-zone renderer accepted')
+print(json.dumps(counts,sort_keys=True))
+print('selftest14:',selftest.check_autozone())

+"""Exercise Qt-independent GUI rules from their source AST, without Qt widgets.
+
+This does not simulate or claim verification of Qt event delivery.
+"""
+import ast, sys, types, itertools, datetime
+from pathlib import Path
+from dataclasses import replace
+
+def isolated(name,path,exclude_classes=(),exclude_assignments=()):
+    tree=ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf8'),path)
+    tree.body=[n for n in tree.body if not (
+        isinstance(n,(ast.Import,ast.ImportFrom)) and getattr(n,'module','') and n.module.startswith('PySide6')
+        or isinstance(n,ast.ClassDef) and n.name in exclude_classes
+        or isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t,ast.Name) and t.id in exclude_assignments for t in n.targets)
+    )]
+    module=types.ModuleType(name)
+    module.__file__=path
+    sys.modules[name]=module
+    exec(compile(tree,path,'exec'),module.__dict__)
+    return module
+
+c=isolated('michspc.gui.controls','michspc/gui/controls.py',('_CalendarOpensOnToday','ObservationDateEdit'),('OBSERVATION_DATE_UNANSWERED',))
+from michspc.gui import options as o, settings_store as ss
+ss.set_store_factory(ss.MemoryStore)
+r=isolated('michspc.gui.results_model','michspc/gui/results_model.py',('ResultsModel',),('AMBER',))
+from michspc import job
+from michspc.fileio import pnezd
+from michspc.spc.frames import NAD83_2011,NATRF2022,WGS84_G1762,WGS84_G2139,WGS84_G2296,ITRF2014,ITRF2020
+from michspc.spc.zones import SPCS83_ZONES,SPCS2022_ZONES
+from michspc.spc.units import METERS,US_SURVEY_FEET
+from tests.fixtures.autozone_anchors import ANCHORS
+
+rendered=0
+for a in ANCHORS:
+    if not a.expected: continue
+    frame=NAD83_2011 if a.system=='SPCS 83' else NATRF2022
+    settings=job.JobSettings(input_path=None,output_directory=None,direction=job.Direction.GEODETIC_TO_ZONE,source_zone=None,target_zone=job.AUTO_ZONE,input_unit=METERS,output_unit=METERS,longitude_convention=job.LongitudeConvention.NEGATIVE_WEST,geodetic_frame=frame,auto_frame=frame)
+    result=job.run(settings,pnezd.parse_lines([f'P,{a.latitude},{a.longitude},,probe\n']))
+    sections=r.single_point_sections(result)
+    text=r.single_point_clipboard_text(sections)
+    point=result.points[0]
+    assert a.expected in text
+    assert point.auto_zone.county_name in text and point.auto_zone.county_fips in text
+    columns=r.columns_for(result)
+    row=r.row_strings(result)[0]
+    assert len(columns)==len(row)
+    assert row[columns.index('Zone')]==point.auto_zone.zone.name
+    assert row[columns.index('County')]==point.auto_zone.county_name
+    rendered+=1
+
+groups=o.LIST_GROUPS
+items=(c.UNCHOSEN,*c.geodetic_choice_groups()[0],None,*c.geodetic_choice_groups()[1],None,*c.AUTO_CHOICES,None,*SPCS83_ZONES,None,*SPCS2022_ZONES)
+combinations=0
+for bits in itertools.product((False,True),repeat=len(groups)):
+    shown=frozenset(g.key for g,b in zip(groups,bits) if b)
+    if o.options_problem(shown): continue
+    for frame,_ in job.AUTO_FRAMES:
+      for source in job.ObservationDateSource:
+        opt=o.Options(shown,frame,source)
+        store=ss.MemoryStore()
+        o.save_options(store,opt)
+        assert o.load_options(store)==(opt,())
+        assert set(store.values)=={*(g.key for g in groups),o.AUTO_FRAME_KEY,o.DATE_SOURCE_KEY}
+        assert all(not isinstance(v,datetime.date) for v in store.values.values())
+        for is_source in [False,True]:
+            flags=o.visible_rows(opt,items,is_source=is_source)
+            seq=[v for v,f in zip(items,flags) if f]
+            assert seq[0]==c.UNCHOSEN and seq[1] is not None and seq[-1] is not None
+            assert not any(a is None and b is None for a,b in zip(seq,seq[1:]))
+            assert not any(c.is_auto(v) for v in seq) if is_source else True
+        combinations+=1
+
+invalids=[True,False,1,0,1.0,[],{},'True','False','1','0','yesterday','NAD83',b'true']
+checked=0
+for key in [*(g.key for g in groups),o.AUTO_FRAME_KEY,o.DATE_SOURCE_KEY]:
+  for value in invalids:
+    store=ss.MemoryStore({key:value})
+    value1,messages=o.load_options(store)
+    assert value1==o.DEFAULT_OPTIONS,(key,value)
+    assert messages,(key,value)
+    assert o.load_options(store)==(o.DEFAULT_OPTIONS,())
+    checked+=1
+assert US_SURVEY_FEET not in c.auto_units(c.auto_choice(NATRF2022))
+assert ss.PRODUCTION_STORES_BUILT==0
+print('pure renderers:',rendered,'anchors; Options:',combinations,'round trips; invalid persisted values:',checked,'PASS')

+"""Invoke specific existing digest checks directly; this is not a pytest run."""
+import sys, types
+from pathlib import Path
+
+class Mark:
+    def __getattr__(self,name):
+        return lambda *a,**kw: lambda f:f
+def fixture(f=None,**kw): return f if f is not None else lambda f:f
+p=types.ModuleType('pytest')
+p.mark=Mark()
+p.fixture=fixture
+p.param=lambda *a,**kw: a
+sys.modules['pytest']=p
+from tests import test_orthometric_regression as orth
+from tests import test_same_frame_outputs_unchanged as same
+from tests import test_frame_disclosure as frame
+root=Path('reviewer_scratch/digest').resolve()
+root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+for name,f in [('orthometric',orth.test_no_default_job_writes_a_different_byte_than_v0_5_0),('same_frame',same.test_no_same_frame_job_writes_a_different_byte_than_before_w4),('frame',frame.test_g2g_reading_no_geoid_is_byte_identical_to_w4)]:
+    d=root/name
+    d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    f(d)
+    print(name,'frozen digest comparison PASS')
+
+from tests import test_autozone_outputs as auto
+for i,(label,tags,system,fr,fields) in enumerate(auto._sweep_jobs()):
+    d=root/f'record_{i}'
+    d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    auto.test_no_one_zone_sentence_survives_on_an_auto_job(d,label,tags,system,fr,fields)
+print('Auto record sentence sweep:',i+1,'single/mixed, vertical, ellipsoid, no-geoid, WGS/ITRF configurations PASS')

+from pathlib import Path
+from dataclasses import replace
+from michspc import job,selftest
+from michspc.fileio import counties
+from tools.build_release import foreign_bundle_files
+
+root=Path('reviewer_scratch/seeds').resolve()
+root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+
+def fails(label,action):
+    try: action()
+    except selftest.SelfTestError as error: print(label,'CAUGHT:',str(error).splitlines()[0][:230])
+    else: raise RuntimeError(label+' incorrectly passed')
+
+original_project=job.project_point
+try:
+    # Auto and fixed agree with each other on raw latitude/longitude instead
+    # of a projection. Only NCAT's independent values can catch this.
+    job.project_point=lambda lat,lon,*a,**kw: replace(original_project(lat,lon,*a,**kw),target_northing=lat,target_easting=lon)
+    fails('no-op projection',selftest.check_autozone)
+finally: job.project_point=original_project
+
+original_data=counties.DATA_DIR
+try:
+    counties.DATA_DIR=root
+    (root/counties.COUNTIES_FILENAME).unlink(missing_ok=True)
+    counties.default_counties.cache_clear()
+    fails('missing county file',selftest.check_autozone)
+    raw=(original_data/counties.COUNTIES_FILENAME).read_bytes()
+    (root/counties.COUNTIES_FILENAME).write_bytes(raw.replace(b'Calhoun',b'Wrong!!'))
+    counties.default_counties.cache_clear()
+    fails('wrong county file',selftest.check_autozone)
+finally:
+    counties.DATA_DIR=original_data
+    counties.default_counties.cache_clear()
+
+bundle=root/'bundle'
+review=root/'review'
+(bundle/'_internal/data').mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
+review.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+(review/'capture.txt').write_bytes(b'reviewer-private-capture\n')
+(bundle/'_internal/data/innocent.bin').write_bytes(b'reviewer-private-capture\n')
+found=foreign_bundle_files(bundle,review)
+if len(found)!=1 or 'innocent.bin' not in found[0]: raise RuntimeError(found)
+print('gate 6 renamed review capture: CAUGHT',found)
+print('restored selftest14:',selftest.check_autozone())

+"""Exercise Qt-independent GUI rules from their source AST, without Qt widgets.
+
+This does not simulate or claim verification of Qt event delivery.
+"""
+import ast, sys, types, itertools, datetime
+from pathlib import Path
+from dataclasses import replace
+
+def isolated(name,path,exclude_classes=(),exclude_assignments=()):
+    tree=ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf8'),path)
+    tree.body=[n for n in tree.body if not (
+        isinstance(n,(ast.Import,ast.ImportFrom)) and getattr(n,'module','') and n.module.startswith('PySide6')
+        or isinstance(n,ast.ClassDef) and n.name in exclude_classes
+        or isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(t,ast.Name) and t.id in exclude_assignments for t in n.targets)
+    )]

+        combinations+=1
